187:void SceneBoss::InitializePhysics()
188:{
189:    m_foundation.reset(PxCreateFoundation(PX_PHYSICS_VERSION, m_allocator, m_errorCallback));
190:    assert(m_foundation && "CRITICAL ERROR: PxCreateFoundation failed!");
192:    m_physics.reset(PxCreatePhysics(PX_PHYSICS_VERSION, *m_foundation, physx::PxTolerancesScale(), true, nullptr));
193:    assert(m_physics && "CRITICAL ERROR: PxCreatePhysics failed!");
195:    // Zero gravity: player Y is clamped manually in Update; no ground plane needed.
196:    physx::PxSceneDesc sceneDesc(m_physics->getTolerancesScale());
197:    sceneDesc.gravity = physx::PxVec3(0.0f, 0.0f, 0.0f);
198:    sceneDesc.filterShader = physx::PxDefaultSimulationFilterShader;
200:    m_dispatcher.reset(physx::PxDefaultCpuDispatcherCreate(2));
201:    sceneDesc.cpuDispatcher = m_dispatcher.get();
203:    m_scene.reset(m_physics->createScene(sceneDesc));
204:    assert(m_scene && "CRITICAL ERROR: createScene failed!");
206:    m_controllerManager.reset(PxCreateControllerManager(*m_scene));
207:    assert(m_controllerManager && "CRITICAL ERROR: PxCreateControllerManager failed!");
209:    m_defaultMaterial.reset(m_physics->createMaterial(0.5f, 0.5f, 0.1f));
210:    assert(m_defaultMaterial && "CRITICAL ERROR: createMaterial failed!");
211:}
213:void SceneBoss::InitializeSubWindows()
214:{
215:    if (!m_windowSystem || !m_player) return;
217:    // =========================================================
218:    // [FIX MUTLAK] Jangan pernah spawn window "player" jika 
219:    // bos sedang berada di Fase Windowkill!
220:    // =========================================================
221:    bool isWindowkillPhase = false;
222:    if (m_navi && dynamic_cast<BossPhase02*>(m_navi->GetCurrentPhase())) {
223:        isWindowkillPhase = true;
224:    }
226:    if (!isWindowkillPhase)
227:    {
228:        // --- Player-tracking window ---
229:        m_windowSystem->AddTrackedWindow(
230:            { "player", "Player", 300, 300, 1 },
231:            [this]() -> XMFLOAT3 {
232:                if (!m_player) return XMFLOAT3(0.0f, 0.0f, 0.0f);
233:                const auto pPos = m_player->GetPosition();
234:                return XMFLOAT3(
235:                    pPos.x + m_stretchOffset.x,
236:                    0.0f,
237:                    pPos.z + m_stretchOffset.y
238:                );
239:            },
240:            [this]() -> XMFLOAT2 {
241:                return XMFLOAT2(
242:                    k_defaultWinSize + m_currentStretch.x,
243:                    k_defaultWinSize + m_currentStretch.y
244:                );
245:            }
246:        );
248:        TrackedWindow* playerWin = m_windowSystem->GetTrackedWindow("player");
249:        if (playerWin && playerWin->window)
250:        {
251:            SDL_Window* sdlWin = playerWin->window->GetSDLWindow();
252:            SDL_SetWindowResizable(sdlWin, true);
253:            SDL_SetWindowBordered(sdlWin, true);
254:        }
255:    }
257:    // --- Register main window with the tracking system ---
258:    // (Lanjutkan kode aslimu di bawah sini...)
260:    // --- Register main window with the tracking system ---
261:    Beyond::Window* mainWindow = WindowManager::Instance().GetWindowByIndex(0);
262:    if (mainWindow && mainWindow->GetSDLWindow())
263:    {
264:        SDL_ShowWindow(mainWindow->GetSDLWindow());
266:        if (!m_windowSystem->GetTrackedWindow("main_window"))
267:            m_windowSystem->RegisterWindow(mainWindow, WindowRole::MAIN_VIEWPORT, m_mainCamera);
268:    }
270:    TrackedWindow* mainTracked = m_windowSystem->GetTrackedWindow("main_window");
271:    if (mainTracked && mainTracked->window && mainTracked->window->GetSDLWindow()) {
272:        SDL_RaiseWindow(mainTracked->window->GetSDLWindow());
273:    }
275:    WindowManager::Instance().EnforceWindowPriorities();
276:}
278:// =========================================================
279:// UPDATE
280:// =========================================================
282:void SceneBoss::Update(float elapsedTime)
283:{
284:    PerformanceLogger::Instance().StartTimer(PerfBucket::Logic);
285:    TimeManager::Instance().Update(elapsedTime);
287:    float activeTimeScale = m_timeScale * TimeManager::Instance().GetHitStopMultiplier();
288:    const float scaledDt = elapsedTime * activeTimeScale;
290:    // =========================================================
291:    // DEATH SEQUENCE LOGIC
292:    // =========================================================
293:    if (m_player && m_player->GetHP() <= 0 && !m_isDying && m_respawnTimer <= 0.0f)
294:    {
295:        StartPlayerDeathSequence();
296:    }
298:    if (m_isDying)
299:    {
300:        m_deathTimer += elapsedTime; 
302:        if (m_deathTimer < DEATH_DELAY_DURATION)
303:        {
304:            m_uberParams.smoothness = FX_BASE_SMOOTHNESS;
305:            m_uberParams.intensity = FX_BASE_INTENSITY;
306:            m_fadeAlpha = 0.0f;
307:        }
308:        else
309:        {
310:            const float fadeTime{ m_deathTimer - DEATH_DELAY_DURATION };
311:            const float t{ std::clamp(fadeTime / DEATH_FADE_DURATION, 0.0f, 1.0f) };
313:            m_uberParams.smoothness = FX_BASE_SMOOTHNESS + (FX_BLACK_SMOOTHNESS - FX_BASE_SMOOTHNESS) * t;
314:            m_uberParams.intensity = FX_BASE_INTENSITY + (FX_BLACK_INTENSITY - FX_BASE_INTENSITY) * t;
315:            m_fadeAlpha = t;
317:            if (t >= 1.0f)
318:            {
319:                ResetLevel();
320:                m_isDying = false;
321:                m_respawnTimer = RESPAWN_FADE_DURATION;
323:                m_uberParams.smoothness = FX_BLACK_SMOOTHNESS;
324:                m_uberParams.intensity = FX_BLACK_INTENSITY;
325:                m_fadeAlpha = 1.0f;
326:            }
327:        }
328:    }
329:    else if (m_respawnTimer > 0.0f)
330:    {
331:        m_respawnTimer -= elapsedTime;
333:        if (m_player) m_player->SetInputEnabled(false);
335:        const float linearT{ std::clamp(m_respawnTimer / RESPAWN_FADE_DURATION, 0.0f, 1.0f) };
336:        const float t{ linearT * linearT }; // Quadratic Ease-Out
338:        m_uberParams.smoothness = FX_BASE_SMOOTHNESS + (FX_BLACK_SMOOTHNESS - FX_BASE_SMOOTHNESS) * t;
339:        m_uberParams.intensity = FX_BASE_INTENSITY + (FX_BLACK_INTENSITY - FX_BASE_INTENSITY) * t;
340:        m_fadeAlpha = t;
342:        if (m_respawnTimer <= 0.0f && m_player)
343:        {
344:            m_player->SetInputEnabled(true);
346:            if (m_navi)
347:            {
348:                if (auto* normalPhase = dynamic_cast<BossPhase01*>(m_navi->GetCurrentPhase())) {
349:                    normalPhase->SetAIEnabled(true);
350:                }
351:                else if (auto* wkPhase = dynamic_cast<BossPhase02*>(m_navi->GetCurrentPhase())) {
352:                    wkPhase->SetAIEnabled(true);
353:                }
354:            }
355:        }
356:    }
357:    else
358:    {
359:        m_uberParams.smoothness = FX_BASE_SMOOTHNESS;
360:        m_uberParams.intensity = FX_BASE_INTENSITY;
361:        m_fadeAlpha = 0.0f;
362:    }
364:    // --- PhysX tick ---
365:    if (m_scene)
366:    {
367:        m_scene->simulate(scaledDt);
368:        m_scene->fetchResults(true);
369:    }
371:    Camera* activeCam = CameraController::Instance().GetActiveCamera().get();
373:    if (m_navi) {
374:        auto* wkPhase = dynamic_cast<BossPhase02*>(m_navi->GetCurrentPhase());
376:        // Trigger the start of the death sequence
377:        if (wkPhase && wkPhase->IsDead() && !m_isNaviDefeated)
378:        {
379:            m_isNaviDefeated = true;
380:            m_naviDefeatTimer = 0.0f;
381:            AddLog("Navi defeated. Starting death sequence.");
382:        }
384:        // Logic for the Fade-In
385:        if (m_isNaviDefeated)
386:        {
387:            m_naviDefeatTimer += elapsedTime;
388:            if (m_naviDefeatTimer > NAVI_DEATH_ANIM_DURATION)
389:            {
390:                float fadeTime = m_naviDefeatTimer - NAVI_DEATH_ANIM_DURATION;
391:                m_whiteAlpha = std::clamp(fadeTime / WHITE_FADE_DURATION, 0.0f, 1.0f);
392:            }
393:        }
394:    }
396:    // Windowkill Phase Logic (Check for Scene Change)
397:    if (m_navi) {
398:        auto* wkPhase = dynamic_cast<BossPhase02*>(m_navi->GetCurrentPhase());
399:        Beyond::Window* mw = WindowManager::Instance().GetWindowByIndex(0);
401:        if (wkPhase) {
402:            // Visibility logic
403:            if (!wkPhase->IsDead()) {
404:                m_autoSyncMainWindow = false;
405:                if (mw && mw->GetSDLWindow()) SDL_HideWindow(mw->GetSDLWindow());
406:            }
407:            else {
408:                if (mw && mw->GetSDLWindow()) SDL_ShowWindow(mw->GetSDLWindow());
409:            }
411:            // ONLY change scene if the White Fade is complete!
412:            if (m_whiteAlpha >= 1.0f && !m_isPendingSceneChange) {
413:                m_isPendingSceneChange = true;
414:                return; // Now it is safe to return/change scene
415:            }
416:        }
417:    }
419:    // --- Sync main window size ---
420:    Beyond::Window* mainWindow = WindowManager::Instance().GetWindowByIndex(0);
421:    if (mainWindow)
422:    {
423:        if (m_autoSyncMainWindow)
424:        {
425:#ifdef NAVI_DEBUG_GUI
426:            int winX, winY;
427:            SDL_GetWindowPosition(mainWindow->GetSDLWindow(), &winX, &winY);
428:            SDL_SetWindowSize(mainWindow->GetSDLWindow(),
429:                static_cast<int>(m_debugPanelSize.x),
430:                static_cast<int>(m_debugPanelSize.y));
431:            m_windowSystem->UpdateWindowBounds(0,
432:                static_cast<int>(m_debugPanelSize.x),
433:                static_cast<int>(m_debugPanelSize.y));
434:#endif
435:        }
436:        else
437:        {
438:            m_windowSystem->UpdateWindowBounds(0, mainWindow->GetWidth(), mainWindow->GetHeight());
439:        }
440:    }
442:    // =========================================================
443:    // KAMERA STATIS (NO ZOOM)
444:    // =========================================================
445:    m_targetZoom = 0.0f;
446:    m_currentZoom = 0.0f;
448:    // Kunci Rasio Piksel ke default agar ukuran dunia stabil
449:    float dynamicPixelRatio = k_pixelToUnitRatio;
450:    m_windowSystem->SetPixelToUnitRatio(dynamicPixelRatio);
452:    // =========================================================
453:    // [FIX] UPDATE KAMERA & CAMERA SHAKE (GABUNGAN)
454:    // =========================================================
455:    // Deklarasi hanya dilakukan SATU KALI di sini!
456:    float newUnifiedHeight = m_windowSystem->GetUnifiedCameraHeight();
457:    auto& camCtrl = CameraController::Instance();
459:    // Ambil nilai getaran (Trauma)
460:    DirectX::XMFLOAT3 shake = camCtrl.GetShakeOffset();
462:    // Set posisi kamera dengan menggabungkan Zoom (Y) dan Shake (X, Z)
463:    camCtrl.SetFixedSetting(DirectX::XMFLOAT3(shake.x, newUnifiedHeight + shake.y, shake.z));
464:    camCtrl.SetTarget({ shake.x, shake.y, shake.z });
466:    // [PENTING] Update kamera menggunakan waktu murni (elapsedTime) agar tetap bergetar saat Hit Stop!
467:    camCtrl.Update(elapsedTime);
468:    // =========================================================
470:    // --- Player update ---
471:    if (m_player)
472:    {
473:        m_player->Update(scaledDt, activeCam);
474:    }
476:    // --- Squash & Stretch ---
477:    if (m_player)
478:    {
479:        const XMFLOAT3 vel = m_player->GetMovement()->GetVelocity();
480:        const float    currentSpeedSq = vel.x * vel.x + vel.z * vel.z;
481:        const float    dashThreshold = m_player->GetDashSpeed() * 0.8f;
483:        XMFLOAT2 targetStretch = { 0.0f, 0.0f };
484:        XMFLOAT2 targetOffset = { 0.0f, 0.0f };
485:        constexpr float lerpSpeed = 10.0f;
487:        if (currentSpeedSq > (dashThreshold * dashThreshold))
488:        {
489:            constexpr float kStretchX = 200.0f;
490:            constexpr float kSquashY = 0.0f;
491:            constexpr float kStretchZ = 0.0f;
492:            constexpr float kSquashX = 0.0f;
494:            const float dashRatio = sqrtf(currentSpeedSq) / m_player->GetDashSpeed();
496:            if (std::abs(vel.x) > std::abs(vel.z))
497:            {
498:                targetStretch.x = dashRatio * kStretchX;
499:                targetStretch.y = dashRatio * kSquashY;
500:                const float signX = (vel.x > 0.0f) ? 1.0f : -1.0f;
501:                // Gunakan dynamicPixelRatio
502:                targetOffset.x = -signX * (targetStretch.x * 0.5f) / dynamicPixelRatio;
503:            }
504:            else
505:            {
506:                targetStretch.y = dashRatio * kStretchZ;
507:                targetStretch.x = dashRatio * kSquashX;
508:                const float signZ = (vel.z > 0.0f) ? 1.0f : -1.0f;
509:                // Gunakan dynamicPixelRatio
510:                targetOffset.y = -signZ * (targetStretch.y * 0.5f) / dynamicPixelRatio;
511:            }
512:        }
514:        m_currentStretch.x += (targetStretch.x - m_currentStretch.x) * lerpSpeed * scaledDt;
515:        m_currentStretch.y += (targetStretch.y - m_currentStretch.y) * lerpSpeed * scaledDt;
516:        m_stretchOffset.x += (targetOffset.x - m_stretchOffset.x) * lerpSpeed * scaledDt;
517:        m_stretchOffset.y += (targetOffset.y - m_stretchOffset.y) * lerpSpeed * scaledDt;
518:    }
520:    // --- Entities & Collision Update ---
521:    if (m_navi) {
522:        // AI Director „Å´„Éó„É¨„Ç§„É§„Éº„ÅÆ„Éá„Éº„Çø„ÇíÊ∏°„Åô
523:        if (auto* normalPhase = dynamic_cast<BossPhase01*>(m_navi->GetCurrentPhase())) {
524:            normalPhase->SetAITarget(m_player.get());
525:        }
526:        // [ËøΩÂä†] Windowkill „Éï„Çß„Éº„Ç∫„Å´„ÇÇ„Éó„É¨„Ç§„É§„Éº„Éá„Éº„Çø„ÇíÊ∏°„ÅôÔºÅ
527:        else if (auto* wkPhase = dynamic_cast<BossPhase02*>(m_navi->GetCurrentPhase())) {
528:            wkPhase->SetAITarget(m_player.get());
529:        }
531:        m_navi->Update(scaledDt);
533:        if (auto* wkPhase = dynamic_cast<BossPhase02*>(m_navi->GetCurrentPhase())) {
534:            if (!m_isPendingSceneChange && wkPhase->IsReadyToChangeScene()) {
535:                m_isPendingSceneChange = true;
536:                //Framework::Instance()->ChangeScene(std::make_unique<SceneTitle>());
537:                return;
538:            }
539:        }
541:        bool isWindowkillPhase = (dynamic_cast<BossPhase02*>(m_navi->GetCurrentPhase()) != nullptr);
543:        if (isWindowkillPhase && !m_playerWindowTransparent) {
544:            // Jika bos baru saja masuk Phase 2, nyalakan transparansi!
545:            m_playerWindowTransparent = true;
546:        }
547:        else if (!isWindowkillPhase && m_playerWindowTransparent) {
548:            // Jika bos kembali ke Phase 1 (atau respawn/mati), matikan transparansi!
549:            m_playerWindowTransparent = false;
550:        }
551:    }
552:    if (m_isPendingSceneChange) return;
553:    if (m_enemyManager) m_enemyManager->Update(scaledDt, activeCam, m_player->GetPosition(), true);
554:    if (m_itemManager) m_itemManager->Update(scaledDt, activeCam);
555:    if (m_collisionManager) m_collisionManager->Update(scaledDt);
557:    EffectManager::Instance().Update(scaledDt);
558:    WindowShatterManager::Instance().Update(scaledDt);
559:    // Terapkan posisi m_fixedPos dan Shakes
560:    //camCtrl.Update(scaledDt);
562:    // --- Sync sub-window cameras to match main camera ---
563:    if (m_windowSystem)
564:    {
565:        for (auto& tracked : m_windowSystem->GetWindows())
566:        {
567:            if (!tracked->isActive) continue;
568:            if (!tracked->camera || tracked->camera == m_mainCamera) continue;
570:            tracked->camera->SetPosition(m_mainCamera->GetPosition());
571:            tracked->camera->SetRotation(m_mainCamera->GetRotation());
573:            //if (tracked->role != WindowRole::SUB_VIEWPORT && m_player)
574:            //    tracked->camera->LookAt(m_player->GetPosition());
575:        }
577:        m_windowSystem->Update(elapsedTime);
578:    }
580:    PerformanceLogger::Instance().StopTimer(PerfBucket::Logic);
581:    const int activeWins = m_windowSystem ? static_cast<int>(m_windowSystem->GetWindows().size()) : 0;
582:#ifdef NAVI_DEBUG_GUI
583:    PerformanceLogger::Instance().EndFrameCheck(ImGui::GetIO().Framerate, activeWins);
584:#else
585:    PerformanceLogger::Instance().EndFrameCheck(0.0f, activeWins);
586:#endif
588:#if !defined(_DEBUG)
589:    // Release keeps the floating window until ImGui is removed (beta).
590:    DrawGUI();
591:#endif
593:    // --- LOGIKA OTOMATISASI OVERDRIVE PLAYER ---
594:    //if (m_player && m_navi)
595:    //{
596:    //    bool shouldUncap = m_forceUncapOverride;
598:    //    // Cek darah boss jika berada di Fase Normal
599:    //    if (auto* normalPhase = dynamic_cast<BossPhase01*>(m_navi->GetCurrentPhase()))
600:    //    {
601:    //        float bossHpPercent = (static_cast<float>(normalPhase->GetHP()) / 1500.0f) * 100.0f;
602:    //        if (bossHpPercent <= m_overdriveBossHpTriggerPercent)
603:    //        {
604:    //            shouldUncap = true;
605:    //        }
606:    //    }
608:    //    // Picu pelepasan batas kekuatan jika kondisi terpenuhi
609:    //    if (shouldUncap && !m_player->IsPowerUncapped())
610:    //    {
611:    //        m_player->ReleasePowerCap();
612:    //    }
613:    //}
614:}
617:// =========================================================
618:// RENDER
619:// =========================================================
621:void SceneBoss::Render(float elapsedTime, Camera* camera)
622:{
623:    Camera* targetCam = camera ? camera : m_mainCamera.get();
624:    auto dc = Graphics::Instance().GetDeviceContext();
625:    auto rs = Graphics::Instance().GetRenderState();
627:    // Detect whether this render call is targeting a transparent sub-window
628:    bool isTransparentWindow = false;
629:    if (m_windowSystem)
630:    {
631:        for (const auto& tracked : m_windowSystem->GetWindows())
632:        {
633:            if (!tracked->isActive) continue;
635:            if (tracked->camera.get() == camera &&
636:                tracked->window &&
637:                tracked->window->IsTransparent())
638:            {
639:                isTransparentWindow = true;
640:                break;
641:            }
642:        }
643:    }
645:    // =========================================================
646:    // [NEW] OVERRIDE CLEAR COLOR UNTUK JENDELA NON-TRANSPARAN
647:    // =========================================================
648:    if (!isTransparentWindow)
649:    {
650:        ID3D11RenderTargetView* currentRTV = nullptr;
651:        ID3D11DepthStencilView* currentDSV = nullptr;
653:        // Ambil Render Target dan Depth Stencil yang saat ini sedang diikat oleh WindowManager
654:        dc->OMGetRenderTargets(1, &currentRTV, &currentDSV);
656:        if (currentRTV)
657:        {
658:            // Bersihkan ulang menggunakan warna kustom dari SceneBoss
659:            float clearColor[4] = { m_clearColor.x, m_clearColor.y, m_clearColor.z, m_clearColor.w };
660:            dc->ClearRenderTargetView(currentRTV, clearColor);
662:            // Wajib di-release karena OMGetRenderTargets menaikkan tingkat referensi (COM AddRef)
663:            currentRTV->Release();
664:        }
666:        if (currentDSV)
667:        {
668:            // Bersihkan ulang depth buffer agar kalkulasi depth 3D tidak rusak
669:            dc->ClearDepthStencilView(currentDSV, D3D11_CLEAR_DEPTH | D3D11_CLEAR_STENCIL, 1.0f, 0);
670:            currentDSV->Release();
671:        }
672:    }
674:    // Transparent windows need TransparentWindow blend state so the alpha
675:    // channel is preserved for UpdateLayeredWindow. Normal windows use
676:    // standard alpha blending.
677:    dc->OMSetBlendState(
678:        rs->GetBlendState(isTransparentWindow
679:            ? BlendState::TransparentWindow
680:            : BlendState::Transparency),
681:        nullptr, 0xFFFFFFFF);
682:    dc->OMSetDepthStencilState(rs->GetDepthStencilState(DepthState::TestAndWrite), 0);
683:    dc->RSSetState(rs->GetRasterizerState(RasterizerState::SolidCullBack));
685:    // =========================================================
686:    // POST-PROCESS VIGNETTE (Only applied to Main Window)
687:    // =========================================================
688:    auto* wkPhase = m_navi ? dynamic_cast<BossPhase02*>(m_navi->GetCurrentPhase()) : nullptr;
690:    // 2. Determine if Windowkill is active AND the boss is NOT dead
691:    bool isWindowkillAndAlive = (wkPhase != nullptr && !wkPhase->IsDead());
693:    // 3. Use the updated boolean to gate post-processing
694:    bool usePostProcess = (!isTransparentWindow &&
695:        targetCam == m_mainCamera.get() &&
696:        m_postProcess &&
697:        isWindowkillAndAlive);
699:    if (usePostProcess)
700:    {
701:        m_postProcess->SetEnabled(true);
702:        UberShader::UberData& activeData = m_postProcess->GetData();
703:        activeData = m_uberParams;
705:        // Turn off unneeded filters just to be safe
706:        activeData.glitchStrength = 0.0f;
707:        activeData.distortion = 0.0f;
708:        activeData.chromaticAberration = 0.0f;
709:        activeData.scanlineStrength = 0.0f;
710:        activeData.bloomIntensity = 0.0f;
711:        activeData.psxEnabled = false;
713:        m_postProcess->BeginCapture();
714:    }
716:    RenderScene(elapsedTime, targetCam, isTransparentWindow);
718:    if (usePostProcess)
719:    {
720:        m_postProcess->EndCapture(elapsedTime);
721:    }
723:    if (m_showGrid && m_primitive3D)
724:    {
725:        m_primitive3D->DrawGrid(25, 1.0f);
726:        m_primitive3D->Render(dc,
727:            targetCam->GetView(),
728:            targetCam->GetProjection(),
729:            D3D11_PRIMITIVE_TOPOLOGY_LINELIST);
730:    }
732:    auto shapeRenderer = Graphics::Instance().GetShapeRenderer();
733:    if (m_showHitboxes)
734:    {
735:        // 1. Gambar Hurtbox Player (Lingkaran Hijau)
736:        // Kita kunci di y=1.0f agar terlihat menonjol di badan player
737:        if (m_player && m_player->GetHP() > 0) {
738:            DirectX::XMFLOAT3 pPos = m_player->GetPosition();
739:            pPos.y = 1.0f;
740:            shapeRenderer->DrawSphere(pPos, 0.3f, { 0.0f, 1.0f, 0.0f, 1.0f });
741:        }
743:        // 2. Gambar Hitbox Peluru Navi (Lingkaran Merah / Hijau)
744:        if (m_navi) {
745:            if (auto* normalPhase = dynamic_cast<BossPhase01*>(m_navi->GetCurrentPhase())) {
746:                for (auto& bullet : normalPhase->GetProjectiles()) {
747:                    if (bullet->IsActive()) {
748:                        DirectX::XMFLOAT3 bPos = bullet->GetMovement()->GetPosition();
749:                        shapeRenderer->DrawSphere(bPos, bullet->GetRadius(), { 1.0f, 0.0f, 0.0f, 1.0f });
750:                    }
751:                }
752:            }
753:            // =========================================================
754:            // [FIX MUTLAK] LOGIKA WINDOWKILL DITEMPATKAN DI SINI!
755:            // =========================================================
756:            else if (auto* wkPhase = dynamic_cast<BossPhase02*>(m_navi->GetCurrentPhase())) {
757:                for (Bullet* bullet : wkPhase->GetProjectiles()) {
758:                    if (bullet && bullet->IsActive()) {
759:                        DirectX::XMFLOAT3 bPos = bullet->GetPosition();
760:                        // Warna Hijau Lime agar kontras untuk peluru mantul
761:                        shapeRenderer->DrawSphere(bPos, bullet->GetRadius(), { 0.0f, 1.0f, 0.0f, 1.0f });
762:                    }
763:                }
764:            }
765:        }
766:    }
768:    Graphics::Instance().GetShapeRenderer()->Render(
769:        dc, targetCam->GetView(), targetCam->GetProjection());
771:    // =========================================================
772:    // FADE SPRITE OVERLAY 
773:    // =========================================================
774:    if (m_fadeAlpha > 0.001f && m_fadeSprite)
775:    {
776:        float screenW = static_cast<float>(GetSystemMetrics(SM_CXSCREEN));
777:        float screenH = static_cast<float>(GetSystemMetrics(SM_CYSCREEN));
778:        if (auto window = Framework::Instance()->GetMainWindow()) {
779:            screenW = static_cast<float>(window->GetWidth());
780:            screenH = static_cast<float>(window->GetHeight());
781:        }
783:        dc->OMSetBlendState(rs->GetBlendState(BlendState::Transparency), nullptr, 0xFFFFFFFF);
784:        dc->OMSetDepthStencilState(rs->GetDepthStencilState(DepthState::NoTestNoWrite), 0);
786:        m_fadeSprite->Render(
787:            dc,
788:            0.0f, 0.0f, 0.0f,
789:            screenW, screenH,
790:            0.0f, 0.0f,
791:            1920.0f, 1080.0f,
792:            0.0f,
793:            0.0f, 0.0f, 0.0f, m_fadeAlpha
794:        );
795:    }
797:    if (m_whiteAlpha > 0.001f && m_whiteSprite)
798:    {
799:        float screenW = static_cast<float>(GetSystemMetrics(SM_CXSCREEN));
800:        float screenH = static_cast<float>(GetSystemMetrics(SM_CYSCREEN));
801:        if (auto window = Framework::Instance()->GetMainWindow()) {
802:            screenW = static_cast<float>(window->GetWidth());
803:            screenH = static_cast<float>(window->GetHeight());
804:        }
806:        dc->OMSetBlendState(rs->GetBlendState(BlendState::Transparency), nullptr, 0xFFFFFFFF);
807:        dc->OMSetDepthStencilState(rs->GetDepthStencilState(DepthState::NoTestNoWrite), 0);
809:        m_whiteSprite->Render(
810:            dc,
811:            0.0f, 0.0f, 0.0f,
812:            screenW, screenH,
813:            0.0f, 0.0f,
814:            1920.0f, 1080.0f,
815:            0.0f,
816:            1.0f, 1.0f, 1.0f, m_whiteAlpha 
817:        );
818:    }
819:}
821:void SceneBoss::StartPlayerDeathSequence()
822:{
823:    if (m_isDying) return;
825:    m_isDying = true;
826:    m_deathTimer = 0.0f;
828:    if (m_player)
829:    {
830:        m_player->SetInputEnabled(false);
831:        m_player->scale = { 0.0f, 0.0f, 0.0f }; // Hide player
833:        // Stop movement sliding
834:        if (m_player->GetMovement()) {
835:            m_player->GetMovement()->SetVelocity({ 0.0f, 0.0f, 0.0f });
836:        }
837:    }
839:    if (m_navi)
840:    {
841:        if (auto* normalPhase = dynamic_cast<BossPhase01*>(m_navi->GetCurrentPhase())) {
842:            normalPhase->SetAIEnabled(false);
843:        }
844:        else if (auto* wkPhase = dynamic_cast<BossPhase02*>(m_navi->GetCurrentPhase())) {
845:            wkPhase->SetAIEnabled(false);
846:        }
847:    }
848:}
850:void SceneBoss::RenderScene(float elapsedTime, Camera* camera, bool isTransparentWindow)
851:{
852:    PerformanceLogger::Instance().StartTimer(PerfBucket::Render3D);
854:    if (!camera) return;
856:    auto dc = Graphics::Instance().GetDeviceContext();
857:    auto modelRenderer = Graphics::Instance().GetModelRenderer();
859:    RenderContext rc{ dc, Graphics::Instance().GetRenderState(), camera, nullptr };
860:    rc.isTransparentWindow = isTransparentWindow;
861:    // --- 1. DETEKSI KAMERA SAYAP (CAMERA FILTERING) ---
862:    bool isWingCamera = false;
863:    if (m_navi) {
864:        if (auto* wkPhase = dynamic_cast<BossPhase02*>(m_navi->GetCurrentPhase())) {
865:            isWingCamera = (camera == wkPhase->GetFXCamera());
866:        }
867:    }
869:    // A. RENDER PELURU (Selalu di semua jendela agar terlihat menembus layar)
870:    if (m_player) {
871:        m_player->RenderProjectiles(modelRenderer);
872:    }
874:    // B. RENDER TUBUH PEMAIN (Kondisional)
875:    if (m_player) {
876:        // Logika bawaan:
877:        bool shouldRenderHere = m_playerWindowTransparent ? isWingCamera : !isWingCamera;
879:        // [FIX MUTLAK] PAKSA RENDER DI SEMUA KAMERA SAAT WINDOWKILL
880:        if (m_navi && dynamic_cast<BossPhase02*>(m_navi->GetCurrentPhase())) {
881:            shouldRenderHere = true;
882:        }
884:        if (shouldRenderHere) {
885:            const XMFLOAT3 pPos = m_player->GetPosition();
887:            // [MODIFIKASI] Bypass pengecekan Sphere jika sedang dikurung!
888:            bool isInView = camera->CheckSphere(pPos.x, pPos.y, pPos.z, 1.5f);
890:            auto* wkPhase = dynamic_cast<BossPhase02*>(m_navi->GetCurrentPhase());
891:            if (wkPhase && wkPhase->IsPlayerCaged()) {
892:                isInView = true; // Selalu render player selama dia di dalam kandang!
893:            }
895:            if (isInView) {
896:                m_player->Render(modelRenderer);
897:            }
898:        }
899:    }
901:    // C. RENDER MUSUH & ITEM ‚Äî tetap skip di wing camera
902:    if (!isWingCamera) {
903:        if (m_enemyManager) m_enemyManager->Render(modelRenderer);
904:        if (m_itemManager) m_itemManager->Render(modelRenderer);
905:    }
907:    // --- 3. RENDER NAVI (termasuk wajah & background) ---
908:    // Saat mati, paksa render di main cam meski isWingCamera false sudah benar
909:    if (m_navi) m_navi->Render(dc, camera);
911:    modelRenderer->Render(rc);
912:    EffectManager::Instance().Render(camera);
914:}
917:// =========================================================
918:// GUI
919:// =========================================================
921:    void SceneBoss::DrawGUI()
922:    {
923:/*        return;*/ // Add this ‚Äî skips all ImGui rendering for release
925:        if (m_isPendingSceneChange) return;
926:        ImGui::SetNextWindowPos(ImVec2(0.0f, 0.0f), ImGuiCond_Always);
927:        ImGui::SetNextWindowSize(m_debugPanelSize, ImGuiCond_FirstUseEver);
929:        ImGui::Begin("WINDOWKILL MASTER CONTROL", nullptr,
930:            ImGuiWindowFlags_NoCollapse | ImGuiWindowFlags_NoMove);
932:        m_debugPanelSize = ImGui::GetWindowSize();
934:        DrawDebugPanel();
936:        ImGui::End();
937:    }
939:    void SceneBoss::DrawDebugPanel()
940:    {
941:        // Why: once the scene change is pending, phase and window state are being torn down.
942:        if (m_isPendingSceneChange) return;
944:        if (ImGui::BeginTabBar("MasterControlTabs"))
945:        {
946:            // ---------------------------------------------------------
947:            // TAB 1: SYSTEM & ENGINE
948:            // ---------------------------------------------------------
949:            if (ImGui::BeginTabItem("System & Engine"))
950:            {
951:                int activeWins = 0;
952:                int sleepingWins = 0;
953:                for (const auto& tw : m_windowSystem->GetWindows()) {
954:                    if (tw->isActive) activeWins++;
955:                    else sleepingWins++;
956:                }
958:            ImGui::Separator();
959:            ImGui::TextColored(ImVec4(0.0f, 1.0f, 0.0f, 1.0f), "Active OS Windows: %d", activeWins);
960:            ImGui::TextColored(ImVec4(0.5f, 0.5f, 0.5f, 1.0f), "Pooled (Sleeping) Windows: %d", sleepingWins);
962:            if (ImGui::CollapsingHeader("System Metrics & Time", ImGuiTreeNodeFlags_DefaultOpen))
963:            {
964:                const float fps = ImGui::GetIO().Framerate;
965:                ImVec4 fpsColor = (fps < 40.0f) ? ImVec4(1, 0, 0, 1) : (fps < 50.0f) ? ImVec4(1, 1, 0, 1) : ImVec4(0, 1, 0, 1);
966:                ImGui::TextColored(fpsColor, "FPS: %.1f (%.2f ms) [cap: 60]", fps, 1000.0f / fps);
968:                static float s_frametimes[90] = {};
969:                static int   s_offset = 0;
970:                s_frametimes[s_offset] = 1000.0f / fps;
971:                s_offset = (s_offset + 1) % IM_ARRAYSIZE(s_frametimes);
972:                ImGui::PlotLines("Frametime", s_frametimes, IM_ARRAYSIZE(s_frametimes), s_offset, nullptr, 0.0f, 33.0f, ImVec2(0, 50));
974:                ImGui::Separator();
975:                ImGui::SliderFloat("Time Scale", &m_timeScale, 0.1f, 3.0f, "%.1fx");
976:                if (ImGui::Button("Reset Time (1.0x)")) m_timeScale = 1.0f;
977:            }
979:            if (ImGui::CollapsingHeader("Window Tracking Config"))
980:            {
981:                if (ImGui::Checkbox("[All] Toggle Topmost (triggers reset)", &m_topmostEnabled)) {
982:                    WindowManager::Instance().SetTopmost(m_topmostEnabled);
983:                    ResetEverything();
984:                }
986:                if (m_navi) {
987:                    if (auto* wkPhase = dynamic_cast<BossPhase02*>(m_navi->GetCurrentPhase())) {
988:                        bool fxClickthrough = wkPhase->IsClickThrough();
989:                        if (ImGui::Checkbox("[ALL] Toggle Clickthrough", &fxClickthrough)) {
990:                            wkPhase->SetClickThrough(fxClickthrough);
991:                            AddLog(fxClickthrough ? "FX Window: Click-through Enabled" : "FX Window: Click-through Disabled");
992:                        }
993:                    }
994:                }
996:                if (ImGui::Checkbox("[Player] Toggle Transparent", &m_playerWindowTransparent)) {
997:                    if (m_playerWindowTransparent) {
998:                        m_windowSystem->RemoveTrackedWindow("player");
999:                        AddLog("Player Window: Removed (Rendering to SFX Layer)");
1000:                    }
1001:                    else {
1002:                        InitializeSubWindows();
1003:                        AddLog("Player Window: Restored");
1004:                    }
1005:                    WindowManager::Instance().EnforceWindowPriorities();
1006:                }
1008:                ImGui::Checkbox("[ImGui] Sync size to main window", &m_autoSyncMainWindow);
1009:                ImGui::Separator();

1749:    void SceneBoss::ResetLevel()
1750:    {
1751:        // Reset Player State (Safe check prevents crashes)
1752:        if (m_player)
1753:        {
1754:            m_player->SetPosition(0.0f, 0.0f, -8.0f);
1755:            m_player->GetMovement()->SetVelocity({ 0.0f, 0.0f, 0.0f });
1756:            m_player->SetMaxHP(m_player->GetMaxHP());
1757:            m_player->SetInputEnabled(false);
1758:            m_player->scale = { 1.0f, 1.0f, 1.0f };
1760:            if (m_player->GetStateMachine()) {
1761:                m_player->GetStateMachine()->ChangeState(m_player.get(), std::make_unique<PlayerIdle>());
1762:            }
1764:            m_player->GetProjectiles().clear();
1765:            m_player->RestoreShootDelay();
1766:            m_player->SetAimLocked(false);
1767:        }
1769:        // Reset Boss 
1770:        if (m_navi)
1771:        {
1772:            if (auto* normalPhase = dynamic_cast<BossPhase01*>(m_navi->GetCurrentPhase()))
1773:            {
1774:                normalPhase->SetHP(normalPhase->GetMaxHP());
1775:                m_playerWindowTransparent = false; // Normal mode = solid player
1776:            }
1777:            else if (auto* wkPhase = dynamic_cast<BossPhase02*>(m_navi->GetCurrentPhase()))
1778:            {
1779:                wkPhase->SetHP(wkPhase->GetMaxHP());
1780:                m_playerWindowTransparent = true;  // Windowkill mode = transparent player
1781:            }
1782:        }
1784:        // Clean up the Windowkill environment
1785:        m_timeScale = 1.0f;
1786:        WindowShatterManager::Instance().Clear();
1788:        // Smart Camera Reset (Instantly snaps during the black screen)
1789:        CameraController::Instance().SetDynamicZoomOffset(0.0f);
1790:        float unifiedHeight = m_windowSystem ? m_windowSystem->GetUnifiedCameraHeight() : 18.0f;
1792:        CameraController::Instance().SetFixedSetting(DirectX::XMFLOAT3(0.0f, unifiedHeight, 0.0f));
1793:        CameraController::Instance().SetTarget({ 0.0f, 0.0f, 0.0f });
1795:        // Force the camera math to finish instantly
1796:        for (int i = 0; i < 60; ++i)
1797:        {
1798:            CameraController::Instance().Update(0.016f);
1799:        }
1800:    }
1802:    void SceneBoss::ResetEverything()
1803:    {
1804:        CameraController::Instance().ClearCamera();
1806:    // 1. Destroy everything (Navi harus hancur sebelum WindowSystem)
1807:    m_navi.reset();
1808:    m_collisionManager.reset();
1809:    m_enemyManager.reset();
1810:    m_itemManager.reset();
1811:    m_stage.reset();

1816:    if (m_windowSystem) m_windowSystem->ClearAll();

1819:    // 2. Rebuild PhysX
1820:    m_defaultMaterial.reset();
1821:    m_controllerManager.reset();
1822:    m_scene.reset();
1823:    m_dispatcher.reset();
1824:    m_physics.reset();
1825:    m_foundation.reset();
1826:    InitializePhysics();
1828:    // 3. Rebuild Window System & Camera
1829:    m_windowSystem = std::make_unique<WindowTrackingSystem>();

1836:    m_mainCamera->SetPosition(0.0f, unifiedHeight, 0.0f);

1846:    m_player = std::make_unique<Player>();

1848:    PlayerConfig bossConfig{};

1850:    bossConfig.dashSpeed = 60.0f;         // Kembalikan nilai dash
1851:    bossConfig.gravityEnabled = false;    // Pastikan gravity mati saat reset!
1853:    m_player->ApplyConfig(bossConfig);
1855:    m_player->SetPosition(0.0f, 0.0f, -8.0f);
1857:    m_stage = std::make_unique<Stage>(device);
1859:    m_collisionManager = std::make_unique<CollisionManager>();
1860:    m_collisionManager->Initialize(m_player.get(), m_stage.get(), m_enemyManager.get(), m_itemManager.get());
1861:    m_player->SetCollisionManager(m_collisionManager.get());

1865:    // =========================================================

1867:    m_navi->Initialize(m_windowSystem.get());

1873:    m_player->SetMaxHP(m_player->GetMaxHP());

1876:    if (m_collisionManager) m_collisionManager->SetBoss(m_navi.get());
1878:    // 5. Finalize
1879:    m_timeScale = 1.0f;
1880:    m_spawnCount = 0;
1881:    m_currentStretch = { 0.0f, 0.0f };
1882:    m_stretchOffset = { 0.0f, 0.0f };

1947:void SceneBoss::CloseSubWindowBySDLID(Uint32 sdlWindowID)
1948:{
1949:    if (!m_windowSystem) return;
1951:    for (const auto& tracked : m_windowSystem->GetWindows())
1952:    {
1953:        if (tracked->window &&
1954:            SDL_GetWindowID(tracked->window->GetSDLWindow()) == sdlWindowID)
1955:        {
1956:            const std::string name = tracked->name;
1957:            m_windowSystem->RemoveTrackedWindow(name);
1958:            AddLog("Window closed: " + name);
1959:            return;
1960:        }
1961:    }

25:using namespace DirectX;
27:namespace
28:{
29:    size_t NextUtf8Offset(const std::string& text, size_t offset)
30:    {
31:        if (offset >= text.size()) return text.size();
33:        const unsigned char lead = static_cast<unsigned char>(text[offset]);
34:        size_t length = 1;
36:        if ((lead & 0x80) == 0x00) length = 1;
37:        else if ((lead & 0xE0) == 0xC0) length = 2;
38:        else if ((lead & 0xF0) == 0xE0) length = 3;
39:        else if ((lead & 0xF8) == 0xF0) length = 4;
41:        return (std::min)(offset + length, text.size());
42:    }
44:    int CountUtf8Characters(const std::string& text)
45:    {
46:        int count = 0;
47:        for (size_t offset = 0; offset < text.size(); offset = NextUtf8Offset(text, offset))
48:        {
49:            ++count;
50:        }
51:        return count;
52:    }
54:    std::string Utf8Prefix(const std::string& text, int characterCount)
55:    {
56:        if (characterCount <= 0) return {};
58:        size_t offset = 0;
59:        int count = 0;
60:        while (offset < text.size() && count < characterCount)
61:        {
62:            offset = NextUtf8Offset(text, offset);
63:            ++count;
64:        }
65:        return text.substr(0, offset);
66:    }
67:}
69:[[nodiscard]] bool SceneGame::CheckPauseToggleTriggered() const noexcept
70:{
71:    // BUG PREVENTION: Do not allow pausing during death, respawn, or boot transitions.
72:    // This prevents soft-locks where timers freeze during critical system states.
73:    if (m_isDying || m_isNaviDefeatSequenceActive || m_bootTimer > 0.0f)
74:    {
75:        return false;
76:    }
78:    auto& input = Input::Instance();
80:    // BUG PREVENTION: Use IsTriggered / GetButtonDown (not IsPressed).
81:    // This guarantees the pause only fires once per physical key press, even if held.
82:    const bool isEscTriggered = input.GetKeyboard().IsTriggered(VK_ESCAPE);
83:    const bool isStartTriggered = (input.GetGamePad().GetButtonDown() & GamePad::BTN_START) != 0;
85:    return isEscTriggered || isStartTriggered;
86:}
88:SceneGame::SceneGame()
89:{
90:    float screenW{ Config::DEFAULT_SCREEN_W };
91:    float screenH{ Config::DEFAULT_SCREEN_H };
93:    if (auto window{ Framework::Instance()->GetMainWindow() }) {
94:        SDL_Window* sdlWin = window->GetSDLWindow();
96:        // Disable window borders and the ability to resize
97:        SDL_SetWindowBordered(sdlWin, false);
98:        SDL_SetWindowResizable(sdlWin, false);
100:        // Grab monitor size and force the window to match it perfectly
101:        int fullW = GetSystemMetrics(SM_CXSCREEN);
102:        int fullH = GetSystemMetrics(SM_CYSCREEN);
103:        SDL_SetWindowSize(sdlWin, fullW, fullH);
104:        SDL_SetWindowPosition(sdlWin, 0, 0);
106:        // Update local configuration variables
107:        screenW = static_cast<float>(fullW);
108:        screenH = static_cast<float>(fullH);
109:    }
111:    auto& camCtrl{ CameraController::Instance() };
112:    camCtrl.ClearCamera();
113:    camCtrl.StopSequence();
114:    camCtrl.SetTargetOffset({ 0.0f, 0.0f, 0.0f });
115:    camCtrl.SetFixedYawOffset(0.0f);
116:    camCtrl.SetFixedRollOffset(0.0f);
117:    camCtrl.SetSplineTension(1.0f);
119:    m_mainCamera = std::make_shared<Camera>();
120:    m_mainCamera->SetPerspectiveFov(XMConvertToRadians(Config::CAM_FOV), screenW / screenH, Config::CAM_NEAR, Config::CAM_FAR);
122:    XMFLOAT3 startPos{ m_cameraPosition };
123:    startPos.x = 0.0f;
124:    startPos.z = -14.0f;
125:    startPos.y = Config::CAM_START_HEIGHT;
127:    m_mainCamera->SetPosition(startPos);

134:    m_stage = std::make_unique<Stage>(Graphics::Instance().GetDevice());
136:    m_foundation.reset(PxCreateFoundation(PX_PHYSICS_VERSION, m_allocator, m_errorCallback));
137:    assert(m_foundation != nullptr && "CRITICAL ERROR: PxCreateFoundation failed!");
139:    m_physics.reset(PxCreatePhysics(PX_PHYSICS_VERSION, *m_foundation, physx::PxTolerancesScale(), true, nullptr));
140:    assert(m_physics != nullptr && "CRITICAL ERROR: PxCreatePhysics failed!");
142:    physx::PxSceneDesc sceneDesc(m_physics->getTolerancesScale());
143:    sceneDesc.gravity = physx::PxVec3(0.0f, Config::GRAVITY, 0.0f);
145:    m_dispatcher.reset(physx::PxDefaultCpuDispatcherCreate(2));
146:    sceneDesc.cpuDispatcher = m_dispatcher.get();
147:    sceneDesc.filterShader = physx::PxDefaultSimulationFilterShader;
149:    m_scene.reset(m_physics->createScene(sceneDesc));
150:    assert(m_scene != nullptr && "CRITICAL ERROR: createScene failed!");
152:    m_controllerManager.reset(PxCreateControllerManager(*m_scene));
153:    assert(m_controllerManager != nullptr && "CRITICAL ERROR: PxCreateControllerManager failed!");
155:    m_defaultMaterial.reset(m_physics->createMaterial(0.5f, 0.5f, 0.1f));
156:    assert(m_defaultMaterial != nullptr && "CRITICAL ERROR: createMaterial failed!");
158:    m_groundPlane.reset(physx::PxCreatePlane(*m_physics, physx::PxPlane(0, 1, 0, 0), *m_defaultMaterial));
159:    m_scene->addActor(*m_groundPlane);
161:    m_player = std::make_unique<Player>();
163:    m_player->SetPosition(m_playerSpawnPos);
164:    m_player->InitPhysics(m_controllerManager.get(), m_defaultMaterial.get());
165:    m_stage->InitPhysics(m_physics.get(), m_scene.get(), m_defaultMaterial.get());
167:    m_player->SetMaxHP(100);
169:    PlayerConfig gameConfig{};

173:    m_player->ApplyConfig(gameConfig);

238:void SceneGame::Update(const float elapsedTime)
239:{
240:    if (CheckPauseToggleTriggered())
241:    {
242:        m_isPaused = !m_isPaused;
244:        // Optional: If you want to pause/resume audio later, do it here:
245:        // if (m_isPaused) AudioManager::Instance().PauseAll();
246:        // else AudioManager::Instance().ResumeAll();
247:    }
249:    if (m_isPaused)
250:    {
251:        auto& input = Input::Instance();
252:        auto& keyboard = input.GetKeyboard();
253:        auto& gamepad = input.GetGamePad();
255:        if (m_isExitingToTitle)
256:        {
257:            m_exitToTitleTimer += elapsedTime;
259:            // Scale perfectly uniform with fade-in configuration
260:            const float t{ std::clamp(m_exitToTitleTimer / RESPAWN_FADE_DURATION, 0.0f, 1.0f) };
262:            // Smooth out the screen using your existing uber shader parameters
263:            m_fadeAlpha = t;
264:            m_uberParams.smoothness = FX_BASE_SMOOTHNESS + (FX_BLACK_SMOOTHNESS - FX_BASE_SMOOTHNESS) * t;
265:            m_uberParams.intensity = FX_BASE_INTENSITY + (FX_BLACK_INTENSITY - FX_BASE_INTENSITY) * t;
267:            if (t >= 1.0f)
268:            {
269:                Framework::Instance()->ChangeScene(std::make_unique<SceneTitle>());
270:            }
271:            return; // Halt logic update securely
272:        }
274:        bool moveUp{ false };
275:        bool moveDown{ false };
277:        // KEYBOARD & D-PAD TRIGGERS
278:        if (keyboard.IsTriggered('W') || keyboard.IsTriggered(VK_UP) ||
279:            (gamepad.GetButtonDown() & GamePad::BTN_UP) != 0)
280:        {
281:            moveUp = true;
282:        }
283:        else if (keyboard.IsTriggered('S') || keyboard.IsTriggered(VK_DOWN) ||
284:            (gamepad.GetButtonDown() & GamePad::BTN_DOWN) != 0)
285:        {
286:            moveDown = true;
287:        }
289:        // ANALOG STICK TRIGGERS (With Latch Protection)
290:        static bool s_analogLatchReset{ true };
291:        const float ly{ gamepad.GetAxisLY() };
292:        constexpr float analogThreshold{ 0.6f };
293:        constexpr float deadzoneThreshold{ 0.2f };
295:        if (ly > analogThreshold)
296:        {
297:            if (s_analogLatchReset) { moveUp = true; s_analogLatchReset = false; }
298:        }
299:        else if (ly < -analogThreshold)
300:        {
301:            if (s_analogLatchReset) { moveDown = true; s_analogLatchReset = false; }
302:        }
303:        else if (std::abs(ly) < deadzoneThreshold)
304:        {
305:            s_analogLatchReset = true;
306:        }
308:        // APPLY MOVEMENT
309:        if (moveUp)   m_uiPause->MoveSelection(-1);
310:        if (moveDown) m_uiPause->MoveSelection(1);
312:        // CONFIRM SELECTION
313:        if (keyboard.IsTriggered(VK_RETURN) || keyboard.IsTriggered(VK_SPACE) ||
314:            (gamepad.GetButtonDown() & GamePad::BTN_A) != 0)
315:        {
316:            const auto selected = m_uiPause->GetSelectedOption();
318:            if (selected == UIPause::PauseOption::Resume)
319:            {
320:                m_isPaused = false;
321:                m_uiPause->ResetSelection();
322:            }
323:            else if (selected == UIPause::PauseOption::Exit)
324:            {
325:                // Turn on the fade-out sequence instead of switching instantly
326:                m_isExitingToTitle = true;
327:                m_exitToTitleTimer = 0.0f;
329:                // Cleanly trigger audio fade out right away
330:                AudioManager::Instance().FadeOutMusic(RESPAWN_FADE_DURATION);
331:                AudioManager::Instance().FadeOutAmbientSFX(RESPAWN_FADE_DURATION);
332:            }
333:        }
335:        return; // Halt the rest of SceneGame::Update while paused
336:    }
338:    m_globalTime += elapsedTime;
339:    if (m_globalTime > Config::TIME_LOOP_MAX) m_globalTime -= Config::TIME_LOOP_MAX;
341:    if (m_navi && !m_navi->IsAlive() && !m_isNaviDefeatSequenceActive)
342:    {
343:        StartNaviDefeatSequence();
344:    }
346:    if (m_player && m_player->GetHP() <= 0 && !m_isDying && !m_isNaviDefeatSequenceActive && m_respawnTimer <= 0.0f)
347:    {
348:        StartPlayerDeathSequence();
349:    }
351:    if (m_isNaviDefeatSequenceActive)
352:    {
353:        m_naviDefeatTimer += elapsedTime;
355:        const float linearT{ std::clamp(m_naviDefeatTimer / NAVI_DEFEAT_FADE_DURATION, 0.0f, 1.0f) };
356:        const float t{ linearT * linearT * (3.0f - 2.0f * linearT) };
358:        m_uberParams.smoothness = FX_BASE_SMOOTHNESS + (FX_BLACK_SMOOTHNESS - FX_BASE_SMOOTHNESS) * t;
359:        m_uberParams.intensity = FX_BASE_INTENSITY + (FX_BLACK_INTENSITY - FX_BASE_INTENSITY) * t;
360:        m_fadeAlpha = t;
362:        if (linearT >= 1.0f)
363:        {
364:            m_uberParams.smoothness = FX_BLACK_SMOOTHNESS;
365:            m_uberParams.intensity = FX_BLACK_INTENSITY;
366:            m_fadeAlpha = 1.0f;
367:            m_isNaviDefeatReadyForNextScene = true;
368:            m_player.reset();
369:            m_navi.reset();
370:            m_enemyManager.reset();
371:            m_itemManager.reset();
372:            m_stage.reset();
373:            m_collisionManager.reset();
375:            // Destroy PhysX core components in reverse order of creation
376:            m_groundPlane.reset();
377:            m_defaultMaterial.reset();
378:            m_controllerManager.reset();
379:            m_scene.reset();
380:            m_dispatcher.reset();
381:            m_physics.reset();
382:            m_foundation.reset(); // <-- This frees PxFoundation for SceneBoss!
384:            // Clear the camera so SceneBoss can claim it without it getting overridden
385:            CameraController::Instance().ClearCamera();
386:            Framework::Instance()->ChangeScene(std::make_unique<SceneBoss>());
388:            return;
389:        }
390:    }
391:    else if (m_bootTimer > 0.0f)
392:    {
393:        m_bootTimer -= elapsedTime;
394:        m_fadeAlpha = 1.0f;
395:        m_uberParams.smoothness = FX_BLACK_SMOOTHNESS;
396:        m_uberParams.intensity = FX_BLACK_INTENSITY;
398:        if (m_player)
399:        {
400:            m_player->SetInputEnabled(false);
401:            CameraController::Instance().SetTarget(m_player->GetPosition());
402:            CameraController::Instance().Update(0.0f);
403:        }
405:        if (m_bootTimer <= 0.0f && m_player)
406:        {
407:            m_player->SetInputEnabled(true);
408:        }
409:    }
411:    else if (m_isDying)
412:    {
413:        m_deathTimer += elapsedTime;
415:        if (m_deathTimer < DEATH_DELAY_DURATION)
416:        {
417:            m_uberParams.smoothness = FX_BASE_SMOOTHNESS;
418:            m_uberParams.intensity = FX_BASE_INTENSITY;
419:            m_fadeAlpha = 0.0f;
420:        }
421:        else
422:        {
423:            const float fadeTime{ m_deathTimer - DEATH_DELAY_DURATION };
424:            const float t{ std::clamp(fadeTime / DEATH_FADE_DURATION, 0.0f, 1.0f) };
426:            // LERP towards pitch black
427:            m_uberParams.smoothness = FX_BASE_SMOOTHNESS + (FX_BLACK_SMOOTHNESS - FX_BASE_SMOOTHNESS) * t;
428:            m_uberParams.intensity = FX_BASE_INTENSITY + (FX_BLACK_INTENSITY - FX_BASE_INTENSITY) * t;
430:            m_fadeAlpha = t;
432:            if (t >= 1.0f)
433:            {
434:                ResetLevel(); // Perform the zero-cost reset
435:                m_isDying = false;
436:                m_respawnTimer = RESPAWN_FADE_DURATION;
438:                // Force screen to stay black for the first frame of respawn
439:                m_uberParams.smoothness = FX_BLACK_SMOOTHNESS;
440:                m_uberParams.intensity = FX_BLACK_INTENSITY;
441:                m_fadeAlpha = 1.0f;
442:            }
443:        }
444:    }
445:    else if (m_respawnTimer > 0.0f)
446:    {
447:        m_respawnTimer -= elapsedTime;
449:        if (m_player) m_player->SetInputEnabled(false);
451:        // Quadratic Ease-Out for a smoother fade-in curve
452:        const float linearT{ std::clamp(m_respawnTimer / RESPAWN_FADE_DURATION, 0.0f, 1.0f) };
453:        const float t{ linearT * linearT };
455:        m_uberParams.smoothness = FX_BASE_SMOOTHNESS + (FX_BLACK_SMOOTHNESS - FX_BASE_SMOOTHNESS) * t;
456:        m_uberParams.intensity = FX_BASE_INTENSITY + (FX_BLACK_INTENSITY - FX_BASE_INTENSITY) * t;
457:        m_fadeAlpha = t;
459:        if (m_respawnTimer <= 0.0f && m_player)
460:        {
461:            m_player->SetInputEnabled(true);
462:        }
463:    }
464:    else
465:    {
466:        // Normal Gameplay Lighting
467:        m_uberParams.smoothness = FX_BASE_SMOOTHNESS;
468:        m_fadeAlpha = 0.0f;
470:        if (!m_hasBGMStarted)
471:        {
472:            AudioManager::Instance().PlayMusic("Data/Sound/BGM_Game.wav", 0.1f, true);
474:            m_hasBGMStarted = true;
475:        }
476:    }
478:    if (!m_hasIntroDialogueTestStarted &&
479:        m_bootTimer <= 0.0f &&
480:        m_respawnTimer <= 0.0f &&
481:        !m_isDying &&
482:        !m_isNaviDefeatSequenceActive)
483:    {
484:        StartIntroDialogueTest();
485:    }
487:    if (m_dialogueBox)
488:    {
489:        m_dialogueBox->Update(elapsedTime);
490:    }
492:    if (m_scene) {
493:        m_scene->simulate(elapsedTime);
494:        m_scene->fetchResults(true);
495:    }
497:    Camera* activeCam{ CameraController::Instance().GetActiveCamera().get() };
499:    if (m_player) {
500:        m_player->Update(elapsedTime, activeCam);
501:        if (m_navi) m_navi->Update(elapsedTime, activeCam);
502:    }
504:    if (m_enemyManager) {
505:        XMFLOAT3 targetPos{ 0.0f, 0.0f, 0.0f };
506:        if (m_player) {
507:            targetPos = m_player->GetPosition();
508:        }
509:        bool canAttack = (m_player && m_player->GetHP() > 0);
510:        m_enemyManager->Update(elapsedTime, activeCam, targetPos, canAttack);
511:    }
513:    if (m_itemManager) m_itemManager->Update(elapsedTime, activeCam);
514:    if (m_collisionManager) m_collisionManager->Update(elapsedTime);
516:    // Furi style cinematic combat camera 
517:    static float targetZoom{ 0.0f };
518:    static int   frameCounter{ 0 };
519:    static const Enemy* cachedClosestEnemy{ nullptr };
521:    if (m_isBossCinematicActive)
522:    {
523:        // 1. Hitung pergerakan kamera (berhenti di angka 1.0)
524:        float t = std::clamp(m_bossCinematicTimer / BOSS_CINEMATIC_DURATION, 0.0f, 1.0f);
525:        float smoothT = t * t * (3.0f - 2.0f * t);
527:        DirectX::XMFLOAT3 currentTarget = {
528:            m_cinematicStartTarget.x + (m_cinematicEndTarget.x - m_cinematicStartTarget.x) * smoothT,
529:            m_cinematicStartTarget.y + (m_cinematicEndTarget.y - m_cinematicStartTarget.y) * smoothT,
530:            m_cinematicStartTarget.z + (m_cinematicEndTarget.z - m_cinematicStartTarget.z) * smoothT
531:        };
533:        CameraController::Instance().SetDynamicZoomOffset(0.0f);
534:        CameraController::Instance().SetTarget(currentTarget);
536:        // 2. State Machine Cinematic
537:        if (m_bossCinematicTimer < BOSS_CINEMATIC_DURATION)
538:        {
539:            // FASE 1: Kamera masih jalan menuju Boss. Timer jalan terus.
540:            m_bossCinematicTimer += elapsedTime;
541:        }
542:        else if (!m_bossDialogueStarted)
543:        {
544:            // FASE 2: Kamera sampai. Munculin dialog dan STOP Timer!
545:            m_bossDialogueStarted = true;
546:            std::vector<std::string> dialogPages = {
547:                u8"„Åà„Å£...Ôºü ‰Ωï„ÅÇ„ÅÆ„Ç≠„Éé„Ç≥...„ÄÇ\n‰ªñ„ÅÆ„ÇÑ„Å§„Çâ„Çà„Çä„ÄÅ„Åö„Å£„Å®Â§ß„Åç„ÅÑ...Ôºü",
548:                u8"„Å°„Çá„Å£„Å®ÂæÖ„Å£„Å¶„ÄÅÊßòÂ≠ê„Åå„Åä„Åã„Åó„ÅÑ„Çè„ÄÇ\n„Å™„Çì„Åã...ËÜ®„Çâ„Çì„Åß„Å™„ÅÑÔºÅÔºü",
549:                u8"„Åç„ÇÉ„ÅÇ„ÅÇ„ÅÇ„ÅÇ„ÅÇ„Å£ÔºÅÔºü\nÊØí„Ç¨„ÇπÔºÅÔºü „Åî„Åª„Å£„ÄÅ„Åí„Åª„Å£...ÔºÅ"
550:            };
551:            // Terjemahan kasarnya:
552:            // 1. Eh...? Jamur apa itu... Jauh lebih besar dari yang lain?
553:            // 2. Tunggu, ada yang aneh. Kok dia... membesar?!
554:            // 3. Kyaaaa?! Gas racun?! Uhuk, uhuk!
556:            m_dialogueBox->StartDialogue(dialogPages);
557:        }
558:        else if (m_bossDialogueStarted)
559:        {
560:            // FASE 3: Dialog sedang berjalan, nungguin player mencet Enter
561:            int currentLine = m_dialogueBox->GetCurrentDialogueIndex();
563:            // Pas nyampe di baris ke-3 (index 2) dan efek belum keluar, TRIGGER RACUNNYA!
564:            if (currentLine == 2 && !m_bossEffectTriggered)
565:            {
566:                m_bossEffectTriggered = true;
568:                static const std::string POISON_SFX{ "Data/Sound/SE_FakeBoss_Poison.wav" };
570:                AudioManager::Instance().PlayAmbientSFX(POISON_SFX, 1.0f, 0.5f);
572:                if (Enemy* fakeBoss = GetFakeBoss())
573:                {
574:                    DirectX::XMFLOAT3 spawnPos = fakeBoss->GetPosition();
575:                    spawnPos.x += m_fakeBossEffectOffset.x;
576:                    spawnPos.y += m_fakeBossEffectOffset.y;
577:                    spawnPos.z += m_fakeBossEffectOffset.z;
578:                    
579:                    m_poisonEffectHandle = EffectManager::Instance().Play(
580:                        "Data/Effect/FakeBossPoison.efk", spawnPos, m_fakeBossEffectScale
581:                    );
583:                    if (m_poisonEffectHandle >= 0) {
584:                        DirectX::XMFLOAT3 rotRad{
585:                            DirectX::XMConvertToRadians(m_fakeBossEffectRotation.x),
586:                            DirectX::XMConvertToRadians(m_fakeBossEffectRotation.y),
587:                            DirectX::XMConvertToRadians(m_fakeBossEffectRotation.z)
588:                        };
589:                        EffectManager::Instance().SetRotation(m_poisonEffectHandle, rotRad);
590:                    }
591:                }
592:            }
594:            // FASE 4: Player mencet enter nutup jeritan Navi. Kotak dialog otomatis hilang, mulai Fade Putih!
595:            if (m_bossEffectTriggered && !m_dialogueBox->IsActive())
596:            {
597:                AudioManager::Instance().FadeOutAmbientSFX(1.5f);
599:                // Jalanin timer lagi buat ngitung efek whiteout
600:                m_bossCinematicTimer += elapsedTime;
602:                float timeInFade = m_bossCinematicTimer - BOSS_CINEMATIC_DURATION;
604:                if (timeInFade < WHITEOUT_FADE_DURATION)
605:                {
606:                    float linearT = std::clamp(timeInFade / WHITEOUT_FADE_DURATION, 0.0f, 1.0f);
607:                    m_whiteAlpha = linearT * linearT * (3.0f - 2.0f * linearT);
608:                }
609:                else if (timeInFade < WHITEOUT_FADE_DURATION + WHITEOUT_HOLD_DURATION)
610:                {
611:                    m_whiteAlpha = 1.0f;
613:                    if (m_poisonEffectHandle >= 0)
614:                    {
615:                        EffectManager::Instance().Stop(m_poisonEffectHandle);
616:                        m_poisonEffectHandle = -1; 
617:                    }
618:                    if (!m_hasHealedForBoss)
619:                    {
620:                        if (m_player)
621:                        {
622:                            m_player->SetMaxHP(BOSS_MAX_HP);
623:                        }
624:                        m_hasHealedForBoss = true;
625:                    }
626:                }
627:                else
628:                {
629:                    // Fade back ke game normal
630:                    float fadeOutTime = timeInFade - (WHITEOUT_FADE_DURATION + WHITEOUT_HOLD_DURATION);
631:                    m_whiteAlpha = 1.0f - std::clamp(fadeOutTime / FADE_BACK_DURATION, 0.0f, 1.0f);
633:                    if (m_navi)
634:                    {
635:                        m_navi->SetPotionedState(true);
637:                        m_navi->StartAttackDelay(999.0f);
638:                    }
640:                    // Final cleanup pas layarnya udah 100% normal (White alpha 0)
641:                    if (m_whiteAlpha <= 0.0f)
642:                    {
643:                        m_whiteAlpha = 0.0f;
644:                        m_isBossCinematicActive = false;
646:                        // Player masih di-lock, Navi belum nyerang
647:                        if (!m_hasTriggeredPoisonDialogue)
648:                        {
649:                            StartPoisonDialogue();
650:                        }
651:                    }
652:                }
653:            }
654:        }
655:    }
657:    else // NORMAL GAMEPLAY CAMERA
658:    {
659:        // 1. Target the Player securely
660:        if (m_player)
661:        {
662:            CameraController::Instance().SetTarget(m_player->GetPosition());
663:            m_director->Update(elapsedTime, m_player->GetMovement()->GetPosition());
664:        }
666:        // 2. Furi style cinematic combat zoom 
667:        static float targetZoom{ 0.0f };
668:        static int   frameCounter{ 0 };
669:        static const Enemy* cachedClosestEnemy{ nullptr };
671:        if (frameCounter++ % 10 == 0)
672:        {
673:            if (m_enemyManager && m_player)
674:            {
675:                float closestDistSq{ 999999.0f };
676:                const DirectX::XMFLOAT3 pPos{ m_player->GetPosition() };
677:                const Enemy* currentClosest{ nullptr };
679:                for (const auto& enemy : m_enemyManager->GetEnemies())
680:                {
681:                    if (!enemy || !enemy->IsActive()) continue;
683:                    const DirectX::XMFLOAT3 ePos{ enemy->GetPosition() };
684:                    const float dx{ pPos.x - ePos.x };
685:                    const float dz{ pPos.z - ePos.z };
686:                    const float distSq{ (dx * dx) + (dz * dz) };
688:                    if (distSq < closestDistSq)
689:                    {
690:                        closestDistSq = distSq;
691:                        currentClosest = enemy.get();
692:                    }
693:                }
695:                cachedClosestEnemy = currentClosest;
697:                if (cachedClosestEnemy)
698:                {
699:                    constexpr float combatRadius{ 25.0f };
700:                    constexpr float maxZoomIn{ -8.0f };
702:                    const float dist{ std::sqrt(closestDistSq) };
703:                    const float intensity{ std::clamp(1.0f - (dist / combatRadius), 0.0f, 1.0f) };
704:                    targetZoom = maxZoomIn * intensity;
705:                }
706:                else
707:                {
708:                    targetZoom = 0.0f;
709:                }
710:            }
711:        }
713:        if (cachedClosestEnemy && !cachedClosestEnemy->IsActive())
714:        {
715:            cachedClosestEnemy = nullptr;
716:            targetZoom = 0.0f;
717:        }
719:        CameraController::Instance().SetDynamicZoomOffset(targetZoom);
720:    }
722:    if (m_player && m_enemyManager && m_dialogueBox && !m_dialogueBox->IsActive())
723:    {
724:        // Cek hanya kalau salah satu dialog belum pernah ke-trigger
725:        if (!m_hasTriggeredMushroomDialogue || !m_hasTriggeredTrackingDialogue)
726:        {
727:            const DirectX::XMFLOAT3 pPos = m_player->GetPosition();
729:            for (const auto& enemy : m_enemyManager->GetEnemies())
730:            {
731:                if (!enemy || !enemy->IsActive()) continue;
733:                const DirectX::XMFLOAT3 ePos = enemy->GetPosition();
734:                const float dx = pPos.x - ePos.x;
735:                const float dz = pPos.z - ePos.z;
736:                const float distSq = (dx * dx) + (dz * dz);
738:                if (distSq < 150.0f)
739:                {
740:                    if (!m_hasTriggeredMushroomDialogue && enemy->GetType() == EnemyType::MushroomNone)
741:                    {
742:                        m_hasTriggeredMushroomDialogue = true;
743:                        StartMushroomDialogue();
744:                        break;
745:                    }
746:                    else if (!m_hasTriggeredTrackingDialogue && enemy->GetAttackType() == AttackType::Tracking)
747:                    {
748:                        m_hasTriggeredTrackingDialogue = true;
749:                        StartTrackingDialogue();
750:                        break;
751:                    }
752:                }
753:            }
754:        }
755:    }
757:    if (m_isPoisonDialogueActive && m_dialogueBox && !m_dialogueBox->IsActive())
758:    {
759:        // Kotak dialog udah hilang karena player mencet Enter di teks terakhir.
760:        m_isPoisonDialogueActive = false;
762:        // Lepas lock input player biar bisa gerak
763:        if (m_player)
764:        {
765:            m_player->SetInputEnabled(true);
766:            m_player->SetAimLocked(false);
767:        }
769:        // Navi langsung nyerang player! (Kasih delay tipis 0.5 detik biar dramatis)
770:        if (m_navi)
771:        {
772:            m_navi->StartAttackDelay(0.5f);
773:        }
775:    }
777:    // Finally, commit all calculations to the actual CameraController
778:    CameraController::Instance().Update(elapsedTime);
780:    if (m_player)
781:    {
782:        m_uberParams.glitchStrength = m_player->GetDamageGlitchIntensity();
783:    }
785:    EffectManager::Instance().Update(elapsedTime);
786:}
788:void SceneGame::StartPlayerDeathSequence()
789:{
790:    if (m_isDying) return;
791:    m_isDying = true;
792:    m_deathTimer = 0.0f;
794:    //AudioManager::Instance().PlaySFX("Data/Sound/SE_Explosion.wav", 0.4f);
796:    // Redundancy: Ensure the player is fully hidden and disabled
797:    if (m_player)
798:    {
799:        m_player->SetInputEnabled(false);
800:        m_player->scale = { 0.0f, 0.0f, 0.0f };
801:    }
802:}
804:void SceneGame::StartNaviDefeatSequence()
805:{
806:    if (m_isNaviDefeatSequenceActive) return;
808:    m_isNaviDefeatSequenceActive = true;
809:    m_naviDefeatTimer = 0.0f;
810:    m_isNaviDefeatReadyForNextScene = false;
812:    AudioManager::Instance().FadeOutMusic(NAVI_DEFEAT_FADE_DURATION);
814:    if (m_player)
815:    {
816:        m_player->SetInputEnabled(false);
817:        m_player->SetAimLocked(true);
818:        m_player->GetMovement()->SetVelocity({ 0.0f, 0.0f, 0.0f });
819:        m_player->GetProjectiles().clear();
820:    }
821:}

827:    if (m_dialogueBox)
828:    {
829:        // Masukkan dialog per halaman/tekanan tombol
830:        std::vector<std::string> dialogPages = {
831:            u8"ÁõÆ„ÇíË¶ö„Åæ„Åó„Å¶„ÄÇÊà¶„ÅÑ„ÅÆÊôÇÈñì„ÅåÊù•„Åü„Çè„ÄÇ\n{ATK}„ÅßÊîªÊíÉ„Çà„ÄÇÈÅ†„Åè„ÅÆÊïµ„ÅØÊíÉ„Å°Êäú„Åç„ÄÅ\nËøë„Å•„Åë„Å∞„Åù„ÅÆÂàÉ„ÅßÊñ¨„ÇäË£Ç„Åè„ÅÆ„ÄÇ",

838:    }
839:}
841:void SceneGame::StartMushroomDialogue()
842:{
843:    if (m_dialogueBox)
844:    {
845:        std::vector<std::string> dialogPages = {
846:            u8"„ÅÇ„ÅÆ„Ç≠„Éé„Ç≥„ÇíË¶ã„Å¶„ÄÇ‰ªä„ÅØÂ§ß‰∫∫„Åó„ÅèË¶ã„Åà„Çã„Åë„Çå„Å©‚Ä¶\nÊ∞ó„ÇíÊäú„Åã„Å™„ÅÑ„Åß„ÄÇ",

852:}
854:void SceneGame::StartTrackingDialogue()
855:{
856:    if (m_dialogueBox)
857:    {
